Second, we note that LLMs enable interactivity, play, and permutation in ways that are seemingly pleasurable for users, raising questions about where AI will fit into contemporary storytelling and entertainment ecosystems. We situate these developments within broader transformations in literature and media, including self-publishing, fanfiction, and pornography, and suggest that AI-generated fiction shares structural affinities with on-demand, personalized, and repetitive cultural forms.

</details>


### [258] [Learning Moral Diversity: Modelling Individual Perspectives in Moral Classification of Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22771)
*Yi Ren,Lewis Mitchell,Matthew Roughan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出在预训练语言模型中融入标注者特定特征层，以建模标注者个体视角，解决道德价值观分类中因主观性导致的标注不一致问题，证明聚合标签会掩盖重要变异并造成性能假象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将多个标注者的标签聚合为单一"黄金标准"，忽视了道德判断任务固有的主观性以及标注者之间因个人观点或文本歧义（尤其是短推文）产生的分歧。

Method: 在预训练语言模型基础上扩展一个学习标注者特定特征的网络层，使模型能够对个体标注者的标注进行预测，而非仅预测聚合标签。

Result: 所提模型提升了对个体标注的预测能力；学习到的表示能揭示标注者道德视角的有意义洞见；聚合标签训练会隐藏标注变异并给出误导性的性能评价。

Conclusion: 标注分歧反映了道德分类任务的内在主观性，对个体视角进行建模能为文本道德分类带来实质益处。

Abstract: Understanding moral values in social media text offers insight into moral judgement formation, and supervised NLP models trained on crowdsourced data have achieved strong classification performance. However, most approaches simplify the problem by aggregating multiple annotators' labels into a single "ground truth", overlooking the inherent subjectivity of the task. In practice, there are disagreements between annotators caused by personal viewpoint or inherent ambiguities, particularly for short tweets. Here, we extend a pretrained language model with a layer that learns annotator-specific features. Our model improves predictions of individual annotations and yields representations that reveal meaningful insights into annotators' moral perspectives. We show that models trained on aggregated labels may hide variation and give a misleading impression of performance. Overall, we demonstrate that disagreement reflects the inherent subjectivity of the task and that modelling individual perspectives creates benefits for moral classification of texts.

</details>


### [259] [Does the Same Token Mean the Same State? MoE Routing as Signal for Reasoning Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22798)
*Kang Chen,Minshen Yu,Junjie Nian,Yaoning Wang,Yixin Cao,Yugang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现稀疏MoE语言模型中，相同token ID并不对应相同的路由状态和专家选择，专家分配仍能区分任务上下文、轨迹历史和推理努力模式。基于此提出RAD（路由一致性解码），一种无需答案字符串的多路径选择器，通过锚定窗口的MoE路由状态进行K近邻密度投票，在10种MoE配置和6个数据集上达到与多数投票相当的性能，并能处理代码生成和智能体补丁选择等无法进行字符串投票的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究稀疏MoE语言模型中路由行为与输出token之间的对应关系，发现相同token ID下路由状态仍存在显著的结构化差异，并利用这一发现设计无需解析答案字符串的测试时控制方法。

Method: 提出RAD（Routing Agreement Decoding）：固定锚点token（如边界锚点或分隔符锚点），用每条路径在锚点窗口内的MoE路由状态作为表示，通过加权Jaccard相似度进行K近邻密度聚类，返回最稠密的路由盆地中心作为最终选择，无需解析、归一化或投票答案字符串。

Result: 在10种稀疏MoE配置（gpt-oss, Qwen3-MoE）和6个数据集（数学、GPQA、代码）上，RAD（73.9%）和RAD+DC（74.2%）与多数投票（73.6%）相当，有微小正差异。在代码生成和SWE-bench智能体补丁选择等无法进行字符串投票的任务上，RAD同样有效。

Conclusion: RAD提供了一种新的接口：在字符串投票合适的场景下性能与多数投票相当，在字符串投票不适用的场景（如代码生成、智能体补丁选择）中也能直接应用，具有更广泛的适用性。但RAD不是验证器，稠密的错误盆地仍可能获胜。

Abstract: In sparse Mixture-of-Experts language models, does the same token id imply the same router state and the same experts producing it? Holding the emitted token id fixed at repeated anchors, we find it does not: the experts that produce it still separate task context, trajectory history, and reasoning-effort mode. This residual structure supports test-time control: near \emph{boundary} anchors (the final-response transition) and \emph{delimiter} anchors (which open the answer, e.g.\ \texttt{\textbackslash boxed\{} or code fences), routing neighborhoods already align with final-answer basins at a marker-only readout and strongest when the routing is read at the answer opening. We operationalize this as \textbf{RAD} (Routing Agreement Decoding), an answer-string-free multi-rollout selector: it locates a fixed anchor, represents each rollout by its anchor-window MoE routing states, and returns the densest Weighted-Jaccard $K$-NN route-basin center, without parsing, normalizing, executing, or voting over answer strings. Across 10 sparse-MoE configurations (gpt-oss, Qwen3-MoE) and 6 datasets spanning math, GPQA, and code, RAD is on par with Majority where string voting is well-posed, with small positive paired deltas (RAD $73.9$ / RAD+DC $74.2$ vs.\ Majority $73.6$). Like majority voting, RAD is not a verifier: a dense \emph{wrong} basin can still win. Its value is the interface: the same selector gives direct pass@1 on code, where exact-string voting is ill-defined, and the same routing-density principle, re-anchored to the agentic boundary, improves best-of-16 patch selection on SWE-bench Verified over random, where patches have no answer string to vote on.

</details>


### [260] [KaLM-Reranker-V1: Fast but Not Late Interaction for Compressed Document Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22807)
*Xinping Zhao,Jiaxin Xu,Ziqi Dai,Xin Zhang,Shouzheng Huang,Danyu Tang,Xinshuo Hu,Meishan Zhang,Baotian Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: KaLM-Reranker-V1 是一种基于编码器-解码器架构的新型重排序模型，通过解耦查询和段落计算实现高效推理，同时利用交叉注意力机制保持丰富的相关性建模能力，在 BEIR、MIRACL 和 LMEB 基准上达到了与强大工业级模型相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重排序模型（无论是编码器还是解码器架构）通常将查询和段落联合编码，导致计算紧密耦合，限制了部署效率和灵活性。因此，需要一种既能解耦查询与段落计算、又能保持表达性相关性建模的重排序方法。

Method: 采用编码器-解码器架构：编码器使用 Matryoshka 嵌入池化预编码段落；解码器处理系统指令、用户指令和查询意图；通过交叉注意力机制捕捉查询上下文与段落表示之间的相关性。这种设计实现了"快速但非延迟交互"（FBNL），即解耦了段落编码但保留了丰富的相关性建模。

Result: 在 BEIR 上达到最先进性能，与 Qwen3-Reranker 系列等强工业模型持平；在 MIRACL 上虽未经过多语言数据大量训练仍表现优异；在 LMEB 上，即使 0.27B 的 Nano 模型也能与 7-12B 的嵌入模型竞争。

Conclusion: KaLM-Reranker-V1 通过解耦查询-段落计算同时保持交叉注意力机制，实现了重排序任务中效率与效果的良好平衡，为大规模信息检索系统提供了高效且灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: As retrieval systems scale, high-quality reranking becomes increasingly important. However, most existing rerankers, whether encoder-based or decoder-based, jointly encode the query and passage, tightly coupling their computation and limiting deployment efficiency as well as flexibility. We present KaLM-Reranker-V1, a fast but not late-interaction (FBNL) reranker that decouples query and passage computation while retaining expressive relevance modeling. Built on an encoder-decoder architecture, KaLM-Reranker-V1 uses the encoder to pre-encode passages with Matryoshka embedding pooling, while the decoder models the system instruction, user instruction, and query intent; cross-attention then captures relevance between the query context and passage representations. This design makes KaLM-Reranker-V1 efficient through decoupled passage encoding, yet not late interaction, by preserving rich relevance modeling through cross-attention. We instantiate KaLM-Reranker-V1 in three sizes, Nano, Small, and Large, with 0.27B, 1B, and 4B activated parameters, respectively. Extensive experiments on BEIR, MIRACL, and LMEB demonstrate that KaLM-Reranker-V1 achieves strong reranking performance with superior efficiency. On BEIR, KaLM-Reranker-V1 achieves state-of-the-art performance, on par with strong industrial models such as the Qwen3-Reranker series; on MIRACL, despite not being extensively trained on multilingual data, KaLM-Reranker-V1 still shows excellent reranking performance. Moreover, on LMEB, reranking models demonstrate a clear advantage, with even the 0.27B Nano model remaining competitive with 7-12B embedding models.

</details>


### [261] [Bagpiper-TTS: Natural Language Guided Universal Speech Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22811)
*Jinchuan Tian,Haoran Wang,Siddhant Arora,Takashi Maekaku,Keita Goto,Jin Sakuma,Yusuke Shinohara,Chao-Han Huck Yang,Shinji Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Bagpiper-TTS是一个通用语音合成系统，通过自然语言提示推理用户意图，生成包含转录和元数据的丰富文本描述，进而合成目标语音，支持多说话人、意图转语音、角色扮演、歌声合成等多种任务，在Seed-TTS-Eval基准上达到1.7%的WER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TTS系统依赖刚性输入格式和预定义元数据槽位，无法满足用户灵活多样的需求。本文旨在设计一个能理解自然语言用户请求并泛化支持多种语音合成任务的通用系统。

Method: 基于自然语言提示，先推理用户意图生成包含转录和元数据的丰富文本描述（rich caption），再以此引导目标语音的合成。

Result: 在Seed-TTS-Eval基准上达到1.7%的词错误率（WER），在LLM-as-a-judge和人类主观评估中，多个应用场景下匹配专用模型的性能。

Conclusion: Bagpiper-TTS作为一种通用语音合成系统，通过意图推理和丰富文本描述生成，成功突破了传统TTS的输入限制，在多种任务上展现了与专用模型相当的性能，验证了该范式的有效性。

Abstract: Classical TTS systems typically rely on rigid input formats and predefined metadata slots, limiting their ability to fulfill flexible user requirements. This paper introduces Bagpiper-TTS, a universal speech synthesis system that deals with diverse natural language user requests. Given a natural language prompt, Bagpiper-TTS first reasons over the users' intent to derive a rich caption, i.e., a comprehensive textual blueprint encompassing both transcription and nuanced metadata. Subsequently, this caption guides the synthesis of the target speech. Our model inherently supports a broad spectrum of tasks besides classical TTS applications, including multi-talker, intent-to-speech, role-play synthesis, singing voice synthesis, and more. Experimental results demonstrate that Bagpiper-TTS achieves an 1.7% Word Error Rate (WER) on the Seed-TTS-Eval benchmark and match the performance of dedicated models in both LLM-as-a-judge and human subjective evaluations across multiple applications.

</details>


### [262] [IndicGuard: A Multilingual Safety Guard Model and Dataset for Indic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22841)
*Parth Bramhecha,Smit Deshmukh,Sairaj Bodhale,Adwait Borate,Raviraj Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IndicGuard，一个针对10种印度语言的多元文化安全防护模型与数据集，通过微调Gemma-3-4B-IT实现实时内容审核，在跨语言安全对齐上显著优于基线模型CultureGuard，并能泛化到未见低资源语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全机制主要针对英语优化，缺乏对印度地区独特的社会文化敏感性及本地化有害类别的覆盖，亟需一个能够捕捉印度语言区多元文化规范的安全防护方案。

Method: 构建涵盖10种印度语言的高容量文化敏感安全数据集（包含区域危害、敏感社会政治上下文及对抗性越狱），在此基础上微调Gemma-3-4B-IT模型，训练为多语言安全护栏模型，用于实时内容审核与策略合规检查。

Result: IndicGuard显著提升了LLM对本地化漏洞的鲁棒性，在多轮对话中保持高度一致性审核；在所有评估语言上一致超越基线模型CultureGuard；并能有效泛化到训练中未包含的低资源印度语言。

Conclusion: IndicGuard证明了面向印度语言的多文化安全对齐框架的结构鲁棒性与跨语言迁移能力，为低资源语言的安全防护提供了可行路径。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve widespread integration across diverse linguistic landscapes, ensuring their safety and alignment with regional normative values remains a critical challenge. Current safety mechanisms are predominantly optimized for English-centric frameworks, often failing to capture the unique socio-cultural sensitivities and localized categories of harm inherent to the Indic region. To address this gap, we introduce IndicGuard, a multilingual safety guard model and dataset for Indic languages. We construct a high-volume, culturally nuanced safety dataset encompassing ten major Indic languages, systematically curated to capture regional harms, sensitive socio-political contexts, and adversarial jailbreaks. Leveraging this corpus, we fine-tune a 4B-parameter instruction-tuned model based on Gemma-3-4B-IT to serve as a multilingual safety guardrail for real-time content moderation and policy compliance checking. Our empirical evaluations demonstrate that IndicGuard significantly enhances LLM robustness against localized vulnerabilities, achieving high moderation consistency across different conversational turns. Crucially, IndicGuard consistently outperforms the existing baseline model, CultureGuard, across evaluated languages. Finally, we demonstrate that our model effectively generalizes to low-resource Indic languages excluded from training, substantiating the structural robustness and cross-lingual transfer capabilities of the framework.

</details>


### [263] [DynamicMem: A Long-Horizon Memory Benchmark in Real-World Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22877)
*Wenya Xie,Shengming Zhou,Zelin Li,Pouya Parsa,Shuang Zhou,Xinheng Ding,Chinmay Arvind,Guanchu Wang,Vladimir Braverman,Ali Payani,Yantao Zheng,Zirui Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DynamicMem是一个合成基准，通过构建每位用户15个月、跨16个应用、平均220万token和1772个事件的长期多模态活动数据，评估LLM代理在异构、随时间演变、隐含表达的用户档案记忆能力。实验发现：档案重建准确率随历史长度下降而服务任务准确率持平；现有系统无法同时保留稳定事实和更新变化事实，错误主要集中在偏好和具体指代；93%以上的失败源于检索而非生成，表明记忆系统本身是最大的改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅通过简短、简化的交互评估LLM代理的记忆能力，忽略了真实用户行为中的三个核心特性：用户档案异构性（属性、习惯、偏好以不同时间尺度演变）；变化由外部语境（如季节、生活事件）驱动；证据很少显式陈述，而是分散在不同应用的细微行为中，需要记忆系统推断。

Method: 构建名为DynamicMem的合成基准，为每位用户生成15个月的跨16个应用（电商、健身、社交等）的活动数据，平均每条轨迹220万token及1772个接地事件。用户档案随时间演变且从未显式给出，每个属性、习惯或偏好必须从跨应用的小信号中推断。设置5个季度检测点评估系统随历史增长的表现。

Result: 测试5个代表性系统发现：(i)档案重建准确率随历史长度下降，但服务任务准确率保持不变；(ii)现有系统无法在保持稳定事实的同时更新变化事实，错误集中在偏好和具体指代；(iii)超过93%的失败源于记忆检索而非模型生成答案。

Conclusion: 最大改进空间在于记忆系统本身，而非生成模型；需要设计能同时处理事实保留与更新的记忆机制。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly act as personal assistants that must remember a user's profile over months: who they are (attributes), what they routinely do (habits), and what they prefer (preferences), and keep it updated as jobs, routines, and tastes drift. Existing benchmarks evaluate this "memory" ability through short, simplified interactions, missing three core properties of real behavior: the profile is heterogeneous, with attributes, habits, and preferences evolving on different timelines; changes are driven by external context such as seasons and life events; and evidence is rarely stated explicitly, instead scattered across many small actions in different apps that a memory system must infer from. We introduce DynamicMem, a synthetic benchmark that constructs 15 months of activity per user, providing long-term multi-app data that real users' privacy keeps out of reach. It provides user-consistent trajectories averaging 2.2M tokens and 1,772 grounded events per user across 16 applications such as e-commerce, fitness, and social platforms. The profile evolves over this period and is never given explicitly: each attribute, habit, or preference must be inferred from small signals scattered across apps. We evaluate at five quarterly checkpoints to track how systems scale as history grows. Benchmarking five representative systems exposes problems a single accuracy score hides: (i) profile reconstruction degrades with history length while service-task accuracy stays flat, despite both drawing on the same memory; (ii) no system both keeps facts that stay true and replaces facts that change, with errors clustering on preferences and on naming the exact referent; and (iii) over 93% of failures trace to what the memory retrieves, not to the model writing the answer, so the largest room for improvement lies in memory itself. Code: https://wenyaxie023.github.io/DynamicMem/

</details>


### [264] [Explanation-Guided Medical Named Entity Recognition with Stability and Boundary Awareness for Atopic Dermatitis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22886)
*Xueguang Li,Di Lin,Xue Jiang,Yanxi Li,Yugang Chi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种稳定性和边界感知的解释引导框架，通过扰动分析、自适应融合局部与全局解释信号，并引入稳定性、边界感知和一致性约束，提升中文特应性皮炎临床文本中医学命名实体识别的可靠性与鲁棒性。实验表明该方法在多个NER模型上取得一致性能提升，并提供了更稳定和边界感知更强的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升中文特应性皮炎（AD）临床文本中医学命名实体识别（NER）的可靠性和鲁棒性，解决现有NER模型在医学领域解释性不足的问题。

Method: 提出稳定性和边界感知的解释引导NER框架，包括：(1) 基于扰动的分析评估解释稳定性和实体边界敏感性；(2) 自适应融合策略动态结合局部与全局解释生成更可靠的token级解释；(3) 通过稳定性、边界感知和一致性约束将融合的解释信号融入模型训练。

Result: 在中文AD NER数据集上，该框架提升了解释鲁棒性，在多个NER模型上取得一致性能提升；自适应融合策略相比单一解释方法提供了更稳定的解释和更强的边界感知能力。

Conclusion: 该方法有效将可靠解释信号融入医学NER训练，同时提升了识别性能和解释可靠性；框架具有实用性和泛化性，为下游临床决策和医学知识应用提供可靠支持。

Abstract: Objective: This study aims to improve the reliability and robustness of medical named entity recognition (NER) in Chinese atopic dermatitis (AD) clinical texts through explanation-guided learning. Methods: We propose a stability and boundary-aware explanation-guided NER framework. Perturbation-based analysis is used to evaluate explanation stability and entity boundary sensitivity. An adaptive fusion strategy dynamically combines local and global explanation to generate more reliable token-level explanations. The fused explanation signals are further incorporated into model training through stability, boundary-aware, and consistency constraints. Results: Experiments on Chinese AD NER datasets show that the proposed framework improves explanation robustness and achieves consistent performance gains across multiple NER models. The adaptive fusion strategy also provides more stable explanations and stronger boundary perception than individual explanation methods. Conclusion: The proposed method effectively integrates reliable explanation signals into medical NER training, improving both recognition performance and explanation reliability. The framework provides a practical and generalizable solution for explainable medical NER and offers reliable support for downstream clinical decision-making and medical knowledge applications.

</details>


### [265] [Understanding Knowledge Distillation in Post-Training: When It Helps and When It Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22942)
*Xin Liu,Simin Ma,Shujian Liu,Song Wang,Sathish Reddy Indurthi,Haoyun Deng,Lu Wang,Kaiqiang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 系统研究了后训练阶段知识蒸馏在大规模指令微调中的应用，发现KD在低数据场景下优于SFT，且强教师模型能带来持续收益；提出了两阶段KD策略用于低资源场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM计算成本高，限制了在资源受限环境中的部署。KD是一种实用解决方案，但先前工作主要关注任务特定或小规模场景，后训练阶段构建通用指令跟随模型的研究有限。

Method: 使用大规模Tulu 3数据集系统研究后训练中的KD；对比KD与SFT在不同数据规模下的表现；分析教师模型强度的影响；提出两阶段KD策略：先用合成教师标注数据，再用人工标注数据精炼。

Result: KD在低数据场景下优于SFT，但优势随数据量增加而减弱；从更强的指令微调教师模型蒸馏即使在数据充足时也能带来显著增益；两阶段KD策略在低资源场景下持续提升学生模型性能。

Conclusion: KD在数据稀缺环境中构建紧凑模型的有效性依赖于教师提供学生从训练数据中难以获取的知识；两阶段KD策略为低资源场景提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across many tasks, but their high computational cost limits deployment in resource-constrained environments. Knowledge Distillation (KD) offers a practical solution by transferring knowledge from a teacher model of a larger size to a smaller student model. While prior work has mainly examined task-specific or small-scale settings, the post-training stage for building general instruction-following models has received limited attention. In this paper, we conduct a systematic study of KD in post-training using the large-scale Tulu 3 dataset. We find that KD outperforms supervised fine-tuning (SFT) in low-data regimes, but its advantage diminishes as more training data is added. Distilling from a stronger instruction-tuned teacher restores substantial gains even with abundant data, indicating that KD remains effective when the teacher provides knowledge that the student cannot easily acquire from the training data alone. We further study domain-specific, low-resource scenarios and propose a two-stage KD strategy that leverages synthetic teacher-labeled data followed by refinement on human annotations. This method consistently improves student performance, providing practical guidance for building compact models in data-scarce environments.

</details>


### [266] [StatABench: Dataset and Framework for Evaluating Statistical Analysis Capabilities of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22977)
*Youxin Zhu,Yixuan Ding,Peng Lai,Longyue Wang,Bingyi Jing,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 StatABench 基准，系统评估 LLM 在统计分析方面的能力。包含 404 道封闭题（Stat-Closed）和 30 道开放建模任务（Stat-Open）。实验表明，即使 GPT-5.1 在封闭题上仅达 68.6%，最佳开源模型为 60.6%，最优智能体框架在开放题上平均得分为 61.86，揭示当前 LLM 与可靠统计分析之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作在统计分析领域对 LLM 的评估存在范围有限、格式单一的问题，缺乏系统性基准来全面衡量 LLM 的统计分析能力。

Method: 构建 StatABench 基准，包含 Stat-Closed（404 题，18 个统计主题，多种题型）和 Stat-Open（30 道复杂开放建模任务，源自专业竞赛）。采用 LangChain MCP 框架和多数据科学智能体评估 LLM，通过 LLM-as-Judge 协议评估 Stat-Open 解决方案。

Result: GPT-5.1 在 Stat-Closed 上仅达 68.6%，最佳开源模型为 60.6%；Stat-Open 上最优智能体框架平均得分 61.86。

Conclusion: 当前 LLM 与可靠的统计分析能力之间仍存在显著差距，在工具推理、方法决策和端到端统计建模方面面临持续挑战。

Abstract: Statistical analysis is a broad, complex field requiring both domain knowledge and tool proficiency. While prior work has evaluated large language models (LLMs) in this domain, existing benchmarks remain limited in scope and format. To bridge this gap, we introduce StatABench (Statistical AnalysisBenchmark), a benchmark designed to systematically assess LLMs' statistical analysis capabilities. StatABench comprises two complementary components: Stat-Closed, containing 404 questions across 18 statistical topics in multiple formats (multiple-choice, fill-in-the-blank, decision-making, and practical application), and Stat-Open, featuring 30 complex open-ended modeling tasks adapted from professional competitions. We evaluate diverse LLMs using the LangChain MCP framework and multiple data science agents, and assess Stat-Open solutions via a validated LLM-as-Judge protocol. Experiments show that even GPT-5.1 achieves only 68.6% on Stat-Closed, while the best open-source model reaches 60.6%. On Stat-Open, the top agent framework scores 61.86 on average. These results reveal the gap between current LLMs and reliable statistical analysis, highlighting persistent challenges in tool-grounded reasoning, methodological decision-making, and end-to-end statistical modeling.

</details>


### [267] [Predicate Importance Estimation and Decoupled Rationale-Score Distillation for Entity Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22992)
*Keunha Kim,Yoonjin Jang,Hyeon-gu Lee,Sihyung Kim,Youngjoong Ko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对工业级KG-RAG系统中异构知识图谱实体对齐（EA）问题，提出谓词重要性估计（PIE）和分离式理由-分数蒸馏（DRSD）两个互补模块。PIE通过去除主语信息、编码无主三元组并聚合可学习的谓词重要性权重，构建谓词感知的实体嵌入；DRSD利用教师LLM生成伪答案，训练小型语言模型（SLM），将二分类EA标签转化为文本监督，并分离置信度评分与标签一致理由。实验表明两者均能提升EA分类性能，且DRSD的分离机制可通过评分与决策的不一致标志不确定预测，实现自动接受与人工验证的实用切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级KG-RAG系统需要整合来自异构数据库的公共知识图谱和领域特定知识图谱，实体对齐（EA）是关键环节。然而，在谓词名称变化和局部邻域不完整的情况下，纯词法匹配无法满足EA需求，亟需更鲁棒的实体对齐方法。

Method: 构建成对EA数据集；提出PIE模块：去除1跳三元组的主语信息，编码无主三元组，通过可学习谓词重要性权重聚合生成谓词感知实体嵌入；提出DRSD模块：利用教师LLM通过不同提示生成伪答案，训练蒸馏SLM，将二分类EA标签转化为文本监督，并分离置信度评分估计与标签一致理由的学习。

Result: 实验表明PIE和DRSD均能有效提升实体对齐分类性能。DRSD的分离机制使得当置信度评分与决策不一致时，可标志不确定预测，从而实现在自动处理与人工审核验证之间的实用切换。

Conclusion: PIE和DRSD两个互补模块有效解决了异构KG集成中的实体对齐问题，尤其是谓词名称变化和局部邻域不完整带来的挑战；DRSD的分离式蒸馏框架不仅提升了对齐性能，还提供了可解释的不确定性标志机制，适合工业级部署中的人机协同验证场景。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) are increasingly used as structured context for Large Language Models (LLMs), but industrial KG-RAG systems often need to integrate public and domain-specific KGs constructed from heterogeneous databases. This integration relies on Entity Alignment (EA), where lexical matching alone is insufficient under predicate-name variation and incomplete local neighborhoods. We address EA for KG integration by constructing a pairwise EA dataset and proposing two complementary modules: Predicate Importance Estimation (PIE) and Decoupled Rationale-Score Distillation (DRSD). PIE is a compact embedding-based approach that removes the subject information from each 1-hop triple, encodes the resulting subjectless triples, and aggregates them with learnable predicate-importance weights to build predicate-aware entity embeddings. DRSD trains a distilled small language model (SLM) with pseudo-answers produced by a teacher LLM through distinct prompts. By converting binary EA labels into text-based supervision and decoupling confidence-score estimation from label-consistent rationales, DRSD enables the SLM to learn task-specific reasoning while retaining a less label-biased confidence signal. Experiments show that PIE and DRSD improve EA classification. Moreover, because DRSD decouples confidence-score estimation from the decision, a discrepancy between the two flags an uncertain prediction for human review, thereby enabling a practical discrepancy between automatic acceptance and human-in-the-loop verification.

</details>


### [268] [Machine Translation and Post-Editing: Comparative Evaluation of Different MT Systems and Post-Editor Groups in Specialised Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23002)
*Joachim Minder,Alexandra Mestivier,Natalie Kübler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了英语到法语专业翻译中机器翻译（DeepL、eTranslation、Systran）和译后编辑的质量，发现三个MT系统和两组译后编辑（语言学家/翻译 vs NLP专家）在术语准确性和流畅性方面存在显著差异，强调了领域知识在专业翻译中的重要性以及MT系统在专门用途语言中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估机器翻译和译后编辑在专业翻译（英语到法语）场景下的质量表现，比较不同MT系统及不同背景译后编辑者的效果差异。

Method: 对比三种MT系统（DeepL、eTranslation、Systran）的输出，并让两组译后编辑者（语言学家/翻译 vs NLP专家）进行译后编辑，使用针对MT和PE评估定制的错误标注类型进行翻译质量评估。

Result: 三个MT系统之间以及两组译后编辑者之间存在显著差异，特别是在术语准确性和流畅性方面。

Conclusion: 强调了领域知识在专业翻译中的重要性，并揭示了MT系统在专门用途语言（LSP）中的性能局限性和可变性。

Abstract: This article aims to evaluate the quality of machine translation (MT) and post-editing (PE) in the context of specialised translation from English into French. Three MT systems (DeepL, eTranslation and Systran) were compared, and two groups of post-editors -linguists/translators and NLP experts -were asked to perform post-editing. Translation assessment is based on error annotation using an error typology adapted to MT and PE evaluation. The results reveal significant differences between the three MT systems and the two groups of post-editors, particularly in terms of terminological accuracy and fluency. This study highlights the importance of domain knowledge in specialised translation, as well as the limitations and variable performance of MT systems in language for specific purposes (LSP).

</details>


### [269] [Have You Ever Seen Them? Entity-level Membership Inference through Interrogating Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23030)
*Yiran Zhu,Ziqi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出实体级成员推断，判断LLM是否使用与目标实体相关的信息进行训练，在仅标签的黑盒设置下通过有限线索构造提示、生成文本并推断成员关系，在人物实体上AUC达0.97。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成员推断研究主要关注样本级别的训练数据泄露，但LLM展现出类似人类记忆的行为：它可能不会逐字记住特定样本，却能从零散提及中积累并泄露关于真实世界实体的知识。因此需要一种实体级别的成员推断方法。

Method: 在仅标签的黑盒设置下，形式化定义了实体级成员推断任务，建立了可行性判定的充要条件，并实例化了五种基于有限线索构造提示、诱导实体相关回复、从生成文本语义特征推断成员关系的询问策略。

Result: 在人物实体数据集上，该方法AUC最高达0.97，平衡准确率比最优基线方法提升6.0%-17.5%。

Conclusion: 实体级成员推断是可行且有效的，能够揭示LLM从零散信息中积累的实体知识泄露风险，为隐私保护和版权合规评估提供了新视角。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) raise growing concerns about privacy leakage and copyright compliance. Membership inference is a key tool for assessing such risks, but existing studies mainly focus on whether specific samples or sample-based data units are used for training. We argue that LLMs exhibit a human-memory-like behavior: an LLM may not memorize a specific sample verbatim, yet it can accumulate and reveal knowledge about a real-world entity from scattered mentions. This analogy motivates us to examine whether an LLM can be interrogated like a human interviewee to reveal its exposure to entity-related information. Motivated by this question, we propose entity-level membership inference, which determines whether information related to a target entity is used in LLM training. We study this task in the practical label-only black-box setting, where only generated texts are observable. We formalize the task under clue, input, and model constraints, establish the necessary and sufficient conditions for its feasibility, and instantiate five interrogation strategies based on this formalization. The strategies use limited entity clues to construct prompts, elicit entity-related responses, and infer membership from semantic features among the generated texts. We construct entity-level datasets and adapt state-of-the-art sample-level label-only methods to the entity-level setting as baselines. Experiments on person entities show that our methods achieve AUC up to 0.97 and bring gains of 6.0%--17.5% in Balanced Accuracy over the best adapted baseline.

</details>


### [270] [Training Open Models for Agentic Phone Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23049)
*Zhengyang Tang,Xin Lai,Pengyuan Lyu,Xinyuan Wang,Tianyi Bai,Chenxin Li,Yiduo Guo,Huawen Shen,Yuxuan Liu,Junyi Li,Zhengyao Fang,Yang Ding,Yi Zhang,Weinong Wang,Xingran Zhou,Liang Wu,Fei Tang,Sunqi Fan,Shangpin Peng,Zheng Ruan,Anran Zhang,Benyou Wang,Ji-Rong Wen,Rui Yan,Chengquan Zhang,Han Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PhoneBuddy提出了一种结合真实应用环境和模拟应用环境（PhoneWorld）的训练方案，用于训练通用型手机智能体。该方案通过共享监督微调、真实环境强化学习以及混合环境强化学习，显著提升了任务成功率（从36.67%提升至45.33%），并证明模拟环境是真实环境RL的互补而非替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实手机环境（运行真实应用）速度慢、有状态、有副作用、难以重置和验证，而可扩展的模拟环境只能近似真实行为，导致训练可靠的手机智能体面临困难。

Method: 构建PhoneWorld模拟环境（从真实GUI使用结构重建可运行的模拟应用）；先在两个环境收集轨迹进行共享监督微调；然后分别进行纯真实环境RL和混合环境（真实+模拟）RL对比。

Result: 在150个真实手机任务的人机评估中，任务成功率从SFT后的36.67%提升至真实环境RL的40.67%和混合RL的45.33%；在AndroidWorld上从60.3%提升至77.2%和83.2%。

Conclusion: 模拟环境训练不是真实环境RL的替代，而是可扩展、可重置、可自动检查的互补来源；增益在应用和小型应用任务上最强，跨应用长流程工作流仍是重要开放挑战。

Abstract: Phones are becoming an important execution surface for general-purpose agents, but training open models for reliable phone use remains difficult because the environment that matters at deployment, real devices running real apps, is slow, stateful, side-effectful, and hard to reset or verify, while scalable mock environments only approximate real behavior. We present PhoneBuddy, a training recipe and open-model line for agentic phone use that combines a real-app environment with a mock-app environment, PhoneWorld, which reconstructs runnable mock apps from real GUI usage structure. PhoneBuddy first builds a shared supervised fine-tuning stage from trajectories collected in both environments, then compares real-app RL against mixed RL across both environments. Across a 150-task human evaluation on real phones spanning apps, mini-apps, and cross-app workflows, task success rate improves from 36.67\% after supervised fine-tuning to 40.67\% after real-app RL and 45.33\% after mixed RL. On AndroidWorld, the same progression rises from 60.3\% to 77.2\% to 83.2\%. These results show that mock-app training is not a replacement for real-app RL, but a complementary source of scalable, resettable, and automatically checked interaction. The gains are strongest on app and mini-app tasks, while long-horizontal cross-app workflows remain an important open challenge.

</details>


### [271] [PIVOTSBench: Evaluating Fine-Grained Interpersonal Relationship Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23092)
*Shuxiang Zhang,Yiting Yin,Wenxuan Song,Yuhang Wu,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PIVOTS基准，用于评估多模态大模型理解双向人际关系维度的能力，包含辅助任务和详细消融实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类天生具有理解细粒度人际关系的能力，但现有MLLM在此多模态推理任务上尚未被充分探索。

Method: 基于Social-IQ 2.0和YouTube数据构建PIVOTS基准，包含双向人际关系维度预测任务和辅助任务（识别关键视觉线索），评估多种MLLM并进行消融研究。

Result: 评估了专有和开源MLLM，分析了视觉模态和社会角色信息的影响，发现联合预测和成对预测设置有助于提升评分效果。

Conclusion: PIVOTS填补了MLLM在细粒度人际理解评估方面的空白，揭示了当前模型的不足与改进方向。

Abstract: Humans possess an innate ability to understand fine-grained interpersonal relationships, which is central to everyday social interactions. Although such reasoning is inherently multimodal, it remains largely unexplored by existing multimodal large language models (MLLMs). To address this gap, we introduce PIVOTS, the first benchmark built from Social-IQ 2.0 and YouTube data to evaluate MLLMs' ability to predict bidirectional interpersonal relationship dimensions grounded in established psychology research. In addition, PIVOTS includes auxiliary tasks that assess models' ability to identify and leverage the critical visual cues underlying such predictions. We evaluate both proprietary and open-source MLLMs and conduct detailed ablation studies to analyze the effects of visual modalities and explicit social role information in conversational utterances. We further examine how joint and pairwise prediction settings benefit MLLMs in scoring bidirectional PIVOTS dimensions. Project page and resources: https://flynnzhangsx.github.io/PIVOTSBench/ .

</details>


### [272] [A Dual-Track Framework for Template-Constrained LaTeX Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23107)
*Chung Cheuk Hei,Liu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种双轨框架（Dual-Track Framework），将模板格式化与文档处理解耦，通过离线轨道提取模板约束为可复用的清单，在线轨道采用混合执行流水线（LLM负责推理密集型任务，规则引擎负责确定性处理），在7个LaTeX模板和56篇论文上实现了更好的结构保真度和编译成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，基于规则的转换器无法正确处理资源插入和模板特定约束，而纯端到端LLM生成易导致语义漂移和幻觉且难以调试。

Method: 提出Dual-Track Framework：离线轨道将模板约束提取为可复用的清单，在线轨道实现混合执行流水线，将LLM限制在推理密集型组件（语义元数据、文献引用、复杂图表布局），规则引擎负责确定性处理。

Result: 在7个LaTeX模板和56篇已发表研究论文上的实验表明，该方法相比基线方法在结构保真度、布局约束满足度和编译成功率上均有提升。

Conclusion: 双轨框架有效地解决了规则转换器和纯LLM生成的各自缺陷，通过合理的任务分工提升了文档转换的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Abstract: With the increasing demands for advanced document conversion, mapping structured Markdown drafts into template-compliant formats like LaTeX remains a challenge. Existing approaches largely depend on either deterministic rule-based converters or pure end-to-end Large Language Model (LLM) generation. The former fails to correctly handle asset insertions and template-specific constraints, while the latter tends to induce semantic drift, leading to hallucinations that are difficult to debug. To address these limitations, we introduce a robust Dual-Track Framework that systematically decouples template formatting from document processing: an offline track extracts template constraints into a reusable manifest, while an online track implements a hybrid execution pipeline. This pipeline confines LLM usage exclusively to reasoning-intensive components (e.g., semantic metadata, bibliographic references, and complex visual/tabular layouts) while delegating rule-based engines for deterministic processing. Empirical evaluation across 7 LaTeX templates and 56 published research papers demonstrates that our method preserves better structural fidelity, satisfies diverse layout constraints, and achieves a higher compilation success rate compared to the previous baselines.

</details>


### [273] [PRIDE: Privileged Information-enhanced Distillation for Empathetic Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23124)
*Jiaqiang Wu,Zhouan Zhu,Shangfei Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PRIDE方法，利用特权信息（训练时可用但推理时不可用的专家标注）进行知识蒸馏，将大模型的共情推理能力迁移到小模型上，在资源受限环境中实现高效共情对话生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在共情对话中表现优异，但计算开销大，难以部署在资源受限环境；现有知识蒸馏方法忽略了共情所需隐含语境线索的迁移，导致蒸馏后模型共情能力不足。

Method: PRIDE包含三个关键组件：(1) 共情推理提示，引导教师模型将共情过程显式分解为理解情感和分析情境；(2) 多源注意力机制，帮助学生模型有效整合特权信息；(3) 双重对齐损失（反向KL散度+最大均值差异），在logit和特征两个层面实现鲁棒知识迁移。

Result: 在多模态和纯文本数据集上的实验表明，PRIDE达到了具有竞争力的性能，在某些情况下甚至匹配或超越了更大的教师模型，在准确性和语义相关性方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 通过引入训练阶段特有的特权信息，PRIDE能够有效将大模型的共情推理能力迁移至小模型，在保持性能的同时大幅降低部署成本。

Abstract: Large language models have demonstrated significant capabilities in generating diverse and context-aware responses for empathetic dialogue. However, their computational demands severely limit their deployment in resource-constrained environments. While knowledge distillation offers a promising compression solution, it often fails to transfer the nuanced understanding essential for empathy, as it overlooks the implicit contextual cues that guide human connection. To bridge this gap, we propose a \textbf{pr}ivileged \textbf{i}nformation-enhanced knowledge \textbf{d}istillation method for \textbf{e}mpathetic dialogue generation (PRIDE). Our method leverages privileged information, such as expert psychological annotations or future event summaries, which is available exclusively during training but unavailable at inference time. This allows us to transfer the teacher model's empathetic reasoning to smaller models without relying on extra inputs during deployment. Specifically, PRIDE has three key components: (1) An empathy-reasoning prompt that guides the teacher to explicitly decompose the empathetic process into understanding feelings and analyzing situations step-by-step; (2) A multi-source attention mechanism that directs the student to effectively integrate privileged information; (3) A dual-alignment loss that combines reversed Kullback-Leibler divergence and maximum mean discrepancy to ensure robust knowledge transfer at both logit and feature levels. Experiments on multi-modal and text-only datasets demonstrate that our method achieves competitive performance, and in some cases matches or even surpasses larger teacher models in terms of accuracy and semantic relevance.

</details>


### [274] [Same question, different history: language, national identity, and credit in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23164)
*William Guey,Pierrick Bougault,Wei Zhang,Vitor D. de Moura,José O. Gomes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大语言模型在回答争议性发明归属问题时，查询语言会系统性影响被提及的 claimants。低地位 claimants 在关联语言提问时出现概率更高，而占主导地位的英语世界人物则跨语言保持稳定。语言充当了切换不同国家历史版本的开关，使同一问题产生系统性不同的国家记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多发明归属存在跨国争议（如无线电、电话、印刷术），以往研究多聚焦历史记录与民族叙事，但语言如何影响大语言模型对争议历史的呈现尚不清楚。本文旨在探究查询语言是否以及如何调节模型对争议发明归属的回答。

Method: 选取11个广泛使用的大语言模型，覆盖21项有争议的发明与发现，在12种语言中进行评估，共收集75,896条响应数据。通过控制响应长度、模型差异、历史知名度和国家纪念水平等变量，分析语言条件对 claimants 被提及概率的影响。

Result: 模型普遍承认归属存在争议，但查询语言系统性影响被提及的 claimant。低地位 claimants 在其关联语言提问时出现概率显著提高，而英语世界的主导性人物（如贝尔、马可尼）跨语言保持稳定。该模式在控制多个混淆变量后依然成立。

Conclusion: 大语言模型构成了分布式文化记忆系统，语言条件决定了哪些历史版本变得可见。这产生了一种计算形式的"平庸民族主义"（banal nationalism），即语言在看似中立的 AI 系统中隐性地塑造了不同国家的历史叙事。

Abstract: Who invented the radio, Russia's Alexander Popov or Italy's Guglielmo Marconi? Was the telephone the achievement of Bell in the United States or Meucci in Italy? Does printing belong to China's Bi Sheng or Germany's Gutenberg? The answer depends not only on historical record but also on language and perspective.
  We analyse eleven widely used large language models across 21 disputed inventions and discoveries, evaluated in twelve languages and 75,896 responses. While models generally acknowledge that credit is contested, query language systematically affects which claimant is surfaced. Lower-status claimants are more likely to appear when questions are asked in their associated language, whereas dominant Anglophone figures remain stable across languages.
  These patterns persist after controlling for response length, model differences, historical prominence, and levels of national commemoration. Language thus acts as a switch that activates different national versions of the same history, producing systematically different national memories from the same question.
  We interpret this as evidence that large language models function as distributed systems of cultural memory, where language conditions which histories become visible, contributing to a computational form of banal nationalism.

</details>


### [275] [When Does Intrinsic Self-Correction Help? A Task-Sensitive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23196)
*Elroy Stav,Dvir Berlowitz,Maayan Orner,Sarit Kraus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出任务敏感视角下的内在自我修正（SC）研究，发现SC在特定任务结构下（如验证显式约束、复现复杂推理过程、为文字游戏提供二次意见）能带来一致性能提升，表明SC的有效性取决于任务类型而非通用可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明模型难以判断自身初始回答是否正确，因此需要探究SC在不同任务中的实际效果及其适用边界。

Method: 采用任务敏感视角，将SC分为三类机制：验证显式约束、复现复杂推理过程、为文字游戏提供二次意见；在多个基准和模型上进行实验评估。

Result: 当任务结构支持上述修正模式时，SC能够带来一致性能提升；否则效果有限。

Conclusion: 内在自我修正应被视为一种任务依赖的推理时策略，其有用性取决于修正阶段在给定任务中能发挥的作用，而非通用的可靠性方法。

Abstract: Intrinsic self-correction (SC) aims to improve large language model outputs by prompting a model to revisit its own initial answer without external feedback. Recent studies have questioned the reliability of this approach, showing that models often struggle to judge whether their initial responses are correct. In this work, we take a task-sensitive view of SC. Rather than asking whether it works in general, we examine settings where SC may operate through different mechanisms: verifying explicit constraints, revisiting a complex reasoning process, or providing a second opinion over competing strategies in word-game tasks. Across multiple benchmarks and models, we find that SC can yield consistent performance gains when the underlying task structure facilitates these modes of revision. These results suggest that SC is best understood as a task-dependent inference-time strategy whose usefulness depends on the role the revision stage can play in a given task, rather than as a uniformly reliable method for improving initial model outputs.

</details>


### [276] [MuPPET: A Benchmark for Contextual Privacy of LLM Assistants in Multi-Party Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23217)
*Elena Sofia Ruzzetti,Cornelius Emde,Sangdoo Yun,Seong Joon Oh,Martin Gubri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MuPPET是一个评估多方对话中上下文隐私泄露的基准测试，实验发现模型在多方场景下的隐私泄露远多于一对一场景，前沿模型存在漏洞，小模型更脆弱，现有防御措施效果有限且会降低实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文隐私基准测试仅考虑单对话者场景，忽视了多方对话中隐私风险的结构性难题——每一条隐私信息必须对所有接收者都合适，缺乏相应的评估方法。

Method: 提出MuPPET（Multi-Party Privacy Exposure Testing）基准测试，用于评估多方对话中的上下文隐私风险，通过实验比较模型在一对一与多方场景下的隐私泄露程度。

Result: 模型在多方设置中的隐私泄露显著多于一对一评估所显示的结果；前沿模型存在漏洞；小规模开源模型（常用于本地敏感数据处理）更脆弱；现有上下文隐私防御仅提供部分保护，会降低实用性，且无法解决根本的参与方追踪问题。

Conclusion: 多方对话中的隐私风险被严重低估，需要专门针对多方场景的隐私保护机制，而不能仅依赖现有的一对一场景防御方法。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed in multi-party environments, handling sensitive personal data on behalf of individual users, for instance in group chats. When such an agent discloses private information, it reaches every group member at once. This risk is structurally harder to control than in one-to-one settings, as every piece of private information must be appropriate for every recipient in the group. Yet all existing contextual privacy benchmarks consider only single-interlocutor settings, leaving multi-party privacy risks unmeasured. We introduce MuPPET (Multi-Party Privacy Exposure Testing), a benchmark for contextual privacy in multi-party conversations. Our experiments show that models leak substantially more in multi-party settings than one-to-one evaluations suggest. Frontier models are vulnerable, and smaller open-weights models, often preferred for local deployment with sensitive data, even more so. Existing contextual privacy defences offer only partial protection, degrade utility, and do not resolve the underlying party-tracking problem.

</details>


### [277] [Judgment-Grounded Expansion for Peer Review Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23233)
*Sheng Lu,Lizhen Qu,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种人机协作的论文评审生成新范式——判断引导扩展（judgment-grounded expansion），即由评审者提供评价性主张，系统将其扩展为完整的评审意见。通过结构化的生成-检查-精炼流程、用户研究和共形预测等方法，解决了可扩展评估和候选集管理两大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全自动评审生成缺乏问责性，不适合需要可追溯和可信赖的场景。作者希望在自动化与问责性之间取得平衡，提出一种人机协作模式，让人类提供核心判断，系统负责扩展细化。

Method: 1) 将任务形式化为结构化生成-检查-精炼流程；2) 开展用户研究收集人机交互数据；3) 设计模拟流程用于大规模评估；4) 应用共形预测（conformal prediction）平衡候选集大小与目标覆盖率。

Result: 共形预测能有效平衡候选评审意见集合的大小与目标覆盖率的控制需求，为可扩展评估提供了方法论支持。

Conclusion: 本文确立了判断引导扩展作为一种具体的任务设置，并为未来协作式评审生成系统的设计提供了实证和方法论基础。

Abstract: Automatic review generation is a promising direction for accelerating scientific progress. While most work adopts an end-to-end setup, its fully automated nature makes it less suitable for settings that demand accountability. To better balance automation and accountability, we formalize judgment-grounded expansion, a human-AI collaboration mode where a reviewer provides an evaluative claim and the system expands it into review comment candidate(s). We model it as a structured generate-check-refine process and conduct a user study to collect human-model interaction data. We study two practical challenges for judgment-grounded expansion: scalable evaluation and candidate set curation. We develop methods to simulate the process for large-scale evaluation, and show that conformal prediction is well suited to balancing candidate set size and target coverage. Our work establishes judgment-grounded expansion as a concrete task and provides empirical and methodological foundations for the design of future collaborative review generation systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [278] [On the Identifiability of User Adaptation in Co-Adaptive Neural Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20569)
*Philip Waggoner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了共适应人机系统中的可辨识性问题，指出闭环编码器估计无法唯一识别用户适应性，而是反映联合系统的特性，并讨论了行为适应性解读的含义及识别条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共适应人机系统中，对用户适应性的估计存在可辨识性问题，即闭环条件下的编码器估计不能唯一地反映用户的真实适应性，这可能导致对系统行为的错误解读。

Method: 通过理论分析框架，研究闭环人机系统中编码器估计的可辨识性，揭示其与联合系统特性之间的关系，并提出确保用户适应性可被唯一识别的条件。

Result: 闭环条件下的编码器估计不能唯一识别用户适应性，而是反映了人机联合系统的共同属性；可辨识性问题源于系统闭环结构引入的耦合。

Conclusion: 为确保对用户适应性的准确解读，需要满足特定的可辨识性条件，否则闭环估计可能产生误导性结论。

Abstract: We analyze identifiability in co-adaptive human-machine systems. We show that closed-loop encoder estimates do not uniquely identify user adaptation, but instead reflect properties of the joint system. We discuss implications for interpreting behavioral adaptation and propose conditions for identification.

</details>


### [279] [Beyond Fixed Budgets: Characterizing the Inelasticity and Limitations of Tree-of-Thought Reasoning Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20599)
*Atkia Mahila,Avinash Maurya,M. Mustafa Rafique,Bogdan Nicolae*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了两种树搜索方法（DPTS和SSDP）在不同计算预算、模型规模和问题难度下的表现，发现DPTS存在冷启动瓶颈（低预算下表现差但高预算下扩展性好），SSDP则因前沿耗尽问题（过早丢弃未探索路径而无法继续改进），表明固定策略无法适应计算资源连续体，需要自适应搜索策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tree of Thought搜索方法在实践中缺乏对不同搜索策略在不同计算预算、模型规模和问题难度下行为的系统研究。

Method: 评估两种代表性ToT方法：基于蒙特卡洛树搜索的DPTS和基于语义去重的SSDP，在两个数学推理基准（Math500和GSM8K）、两个模型规模（Llama-3B和Llama-8B）和四个token预算（3k-10k）下进行对比分析。

Result: DPTS存在冷启动瓶颈，低预算下因探索不足导致价值估计不可靠；SSDP存在前沿耗尽问题，激进的节点合并导致永久丢弃未探索路径，无法随预算增加而改进。

Conclusion: 固定探索策略和固定剪枝策略均不足以适应计算资源连续体，科学推理代理需要能够根据搜索进度和可用资源自适应调整行为的搜索策略。

Abstract: Tree of Thought (ToT) search has become a promising direction for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but deploying these methods in practice raises a question that has received little systematic attention: how do different search strategies behave under varying compute budgets, model sizes, and problem difficulties? In this work, we evaluate two representative ToT methods; DPTS, a Monte Carlo tree search based approach, and SSDP, a semantic deduplication based approach, across two mathematical reasoning benchmarks (Math500 and GSM8K), two model scales (Llama-3B and Llama-8B), and four token budgets (3k--10k). Our analysis reveals that the two methods exhibit limitations that pull in opposite directions. DPTS suffers from a cold-start bottleneck at low budgets: it requires sufficient exploration before its value estimates become reliable, making it a poor fit for resource-constrained settings despite strong scaling behavior at higher budgets. SSDP, on the other hand, reaches candidate solutions efficiently but is prone to frontier depletion; its aggressive node merging permanently discards unexplored paths, leaving it unable to improve regardless of how much budget remains. Together, these findings suggest that neither a fixed exploration strategy nor a fixed pruning strategy is sufficient across compute continuum. We argue that effective search for scientific reasoning agents requires strategies that can adapt their behavior based on search progress and available resources.

</details>


### [280] [The New Associationism: Lessons from Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20600)
*Daniel Rothschild*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现代AI的成功（尤其是监督学习）支持了一种温和的联想主义观点：一种统一的、渐进的、错误驱动的学习机制在多个领域运作，但深度学习架构远超经典联想主义的设想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨现代AI的成功对人类学习机制的解释意义，特别是挑战"联想主义机制过于有限而无法解释人类认知能力"这一传统论点。

Method: 通过对当代AI系统（从大语言模型到游戏智能体）的分析，论证监督学习（基于评估反馈的学习）是其核心机制，差异仅在于产生反馈信号所需的工作量不同。

Result: 监督学习作为一种统一的、渐进的、错误驱动的学习机制，在广泛领域中运作，这为联想主义理念提供了辩护；但深度学习架构远超经典联想主义的范畴。

Conclusion: 现代AI的成功支持了一种温和的联想主义：监督学习作为核心机制是有效的，但它只是深度学习系统中的一个组成部分，而非学习的完整解释。

Abstract: What can the success of modern AI tell us about how humans learn? This paper argues that taking AI seriously as a model of human learning supports a modest but genuine associationism. The central finding is that supervised learning -- learning driven by evaluative feedback -- underlies a surprisingly wide range of contemporary AI systems, from large language models to game-playing agents, differing primarily in how much work is required to generate the relevant feedback signal. This vindicates associationist ideals of a uniform, gradual, error-driven learning mechanism operating across domains, and defuses the once-influential argument that associationist mechanisms are too limited to account for human cognitive capacities. At the same time, the successes of deep learning depend on computational architectures that go well beyond anything classical associationists envisaged, and supervised learning operates within these as one component rather than a complete account of learning.

</details>


### [281] [Specifying AI-SDLC Processes: A Protocol Language for Human-Agent Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20615)
*Ylli Prifti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于AI-SDLC流程规范的领域特定语言（DSL），通过形式化语法、良构条件、操作语义和执行不变量来区分策略与机制，并利用结构强制约束来限制流程非确定性。研究通过故障率分析、2+N团队模式等形式化结果展示了该语言的有效性，并提供了工作原型实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理参与软件开发生命周期（SDLC）时缺乏表达能力人机责任边界、审批门控和治理约束的规范语言；现有方法要么将流程编码在易漂移的代理提示中，要么仅针对相邻领域（工作流管理、业务流程）或碎片化问题（访问控制、审批门控），未能提供统一的正式规范方案。

Method: 提出了一种领域特定语言（DSL），其核心特征包括：(1) 形式化语法、良构条件、操作语义和执行不变量；(2) 区分策略（声明意图）与机制（结构强制）；(3) 通过验证令牌和能力边界等原语约束流程非确定性；(4) 将2+N团队模式（两个人类控制角色+N个专业代理成员）形式化为AI-SDLC的经典职责分离模式。

Result: 三个主要结果：(1) 故障率分析表明结构强制将系统故障率限制在代理与验证器速率的加权乘积，而行为合规则导致累积或近饱和增长；(2) 2+N团队模式形式化了AI-SDLC中的经典职责分离；(3) 编排循环的Kleene闭包和反射性协议合规验证成为设计属性而非特例构造。

Conclusion: 该工作的新颖性在于特定集成而非单一原语，相较于MetaGPT、FlowAgent/BPMN扩展和能力安全（SAGA）等框架具有独特定位；提供了工作原型验证可行性，实证评估为未来工作。

Abstract: AI agents now participate as first-class team members across the software development lifecycle, yet no specification language exists for expressing the human-agent responsibility boundaries, approval gates, and governance constraints this collaboration requires. Existing approaches encode process in agent prompts (subject to drift), target adjacent domains (workflow management, business processes), or address only fragments (access control, approval gates).
  We propose a domain-specific language for specifying AI-SDLC processes as protocols, with formal syntax, well-formedness conditions, operational semantics, and enforcement invariants. The language distinguishes policy (declared intent) from mechanism (structural enforcement), enabling implementations to bound process non-determinism through primitives such as validation tokens and capability boundaries.
  Three results follow. A failure rate analysis shows that structural enforcement bounds system failure rates at a weighted product of agent and validator rates, while behavioral compliance permits cumulative or near-saturating growth. The 2+N team pattern (two human-in-control roles plus N specialized agent members) formalizes classical Separation of Duties for AI-SDLC. Kleene closure of orchestration loops and reflexive protocol-adherence validation emerge as design properties rather than special-case constructs.
  We position the contribution against multi-agent frameworks (MetaGPT), workflow specification (FlowAgent, BPMN extensions), and capability-based security (SAGA): the novelty lies in the specific integration, not any single primitive. A working implementation demonstrates feasibility; empirical evaluation is future work.

</details>


### [282] [PEAR: Permutation-Equivariant Adaptive Routing Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20621)
*Yang Feng,Ziwei Xu,Xia Hu,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PEAR是一种排列等变自适应路由多智能体辩论协议，通过动态重新配置通信角色和稀疏拓扑，防止固定位置偏差，在四个推理基准和六个LLM骨干上显著优于现有辩论方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固定拓扑的多智能体辩论存在持续的位置偏差、放大不可靠智能体的影响以及对角色分配高度敏感等问题。

Method: 提出排列等变自适应路由多智能体辩论协议，在连续辩论轮次中基于智能体状态动态切换角色分配并调整稀疏拓扑，作为等变稀疏路由器降低路由复杂度并提升泛化能力。

Result: 在四个推理基准和六个不同LLM骨干上的综合实验表明，PEAR的平均准确率显著优于最强的辩论基线方法。

Conclusion: PEAR通过动态角色分配和稀疏拓扑重配置有效克服了固定拓扑的固有问题，提升了多智能体辩论的可靠性和准确率。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate improves the reliability of large language models (LLMs) through iterative peer critiques. However, fixed topologies often introduce persistent positional biases, amplify unreliable agents, and cause high sensitivity to role assignments. We introduce \textit{Permutation-Equivariant Adaptive Routing Multi-Agent Debate (PEAR)}, an inference-time protocol that dynamically reconfigures communication roles and sparse topologies across consecutive debate rounds. By strategically switching agent-to-role assignments based on evolving agent states, PEAR prevents any agent from permanently occupying a privileged network position or distributes influence more evenly across the debate. We theoretically characterize PEAR as an equivariant sparse router: it preserves accuracy under agent relabeling while reducing routing complexity and improving generalization. Comprehensive empirical evaluations across four reasoning benchmarks and six diverse LLM backbones demonstrate PEAR significantly improves average accuracy over the strongest debate baselines. The code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/PEAR.

</details>


### [283] [Darwin Mobile Agent: A Roadmap for Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20622)
*Daniel Beechey,Derek Yuen,Jianheng Liu,Dezhao Luo,Tiantian He,Weilin Luo,Jun Wang,Kun Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过移动GUI作为"大世界"代理，并构建Darwin Mobile Agent开源基础设施，通过去除人类先验知识，实现自主强化学习的GUI智能体。核心贡献包括异步并行云手机框架和三条去除人类先验的技术路线图（任务课程、结果验证、记忆管理）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体依赖大量人类先验知识（如人工标注、预设任务、人工验证），限制了其向通用自适应行为发展。作者受"苦涩教训"启发，主张通过消除人类先验、让智能体在复杂环境中与生俱来的方式自然涌现智能。

Method: 1) 提出移动GUI作为"大世界"代理环境；2) 构建Darwin Mobile Agent开源框架，采用异步智能体-环境循环架构，在并行云手机实例上进行数据收集和强化学习；3) 提出三阶段路线图：去除任务课程中的人工先验、去除结果验证中的人工先验、去除记忆管理中的人工先验；4) 验证Darwin基础设施在GUI域策略优化中的稳定性和可扩展性。

Result: 验证了Darwin基础设施在GUI域策略优化的第一阶段（去除任务课程人工先验）中具备所需的稳定性和可扩展性，为后续完全自主的自进化智能体奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 移动GUI是通往通用自适应智能体的有效代理环境；通过系统性地去除人类先验，结合"大世界"交互，可以实现真正的自主自进化GUI智能体；Darwin框架为此提供了必要的实践和理论基础。

Abstract: The goal of artificial intelligence is to create agents capable of general, adaptive behaviour in open-ended environments. Guided by the "Bitter Lesson", we argue that the most effective path toward this goal is to systematically remove human priors and allow intelligence to naturally emerge through interaction with a "Big World" that is orders of magnitude more complex than the agent itself. We propose the mobile Graphical User Interface (GUI) as a practical proxy for such a world and introduce Darwin Mobile Agent, an open-source infrastructure designed as a foundation for autonomous reinforcement learning in this domain. This framework addresses the data-collection bottleneck in real-world mobile interactions by using an asynchronous agent-environment loop across parallel cloud-phone instances. We further propose a conceptual roadmap to systematically remove human priors from three fundamental pillars of a self-evolving agent: task curricula, outcome verification, and memory management. We validate that the Darwin infrastructure provides the stability and scalability required for the first stage of this roadmap: policy optimisation in the GUI domain. This work establishes the practical and theoretical foundation necessary to move toward truly autonomous, self-evolving GUI agents.

</details>


### [284] [Path-dependent program induction under resource constraints explains human sequence learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20623)
*Hanqi Zhou,David G. Nagy,Peter Dayan,Charley M. Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文整合率失真理论与程序归纳，提出分层适应文法（HAG）模型，解释人类如何在有限认知资源下从序列经验中构建抽象、可复用的知识。实验表明HAG在率失真权衡和泛化上优于固定文法，在线旋律学习实验验证了层级结构对记忆和续写行为的解释力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索人类如何在有限认知资源约束下，从顺序经验中构建抽象且可复用的知识。

Method: 将率失真理论与程序归纳相结合，提出分层适应文法（HAG）模型，包含局部（任务内）和全局（跨任务）两层库，受记忆和计算约束共同调控。

Result: HAG在率失真权衡和泛化能力上优于固定文法或浅层组块方法；在线旋律序列学习实验中，参与者的回忆错误呈现系统性简化，反应时在推断的程序边界处增加；逐试次拟合显示层级库模型最佳解释了个体在回忆和续写选择上的差异。

Conclusion: 结构化学习可被理解为有界程序归纳，经验的顺序影响学习者未来构建的抽象结构。

Abstract: How do people build abstract, reusable knowledge from sequential experience under bounded cognitive resources? To answer this question, we integrate rate-distortion theory with recent advances in program induction to describe how prior knowledge shapes which future structures are cheap to encode and easy to discover. We formalize this in a hierarchical Adaptor Grammar (HAG) with distinct local (within-task) and global (across-task) libraries, governed jointly by constraints on memory and computation. In simulations, HAG achieves better rate-distortion trade-offs and stronger generalization than fixed grammars or shallow chunking methods. In an online melodic sequence-learning experiment, participants' recall errors reflected systematic simplifications and reaction times increased at inferred program boundaries. Trial-by-trial fits further showed that hierarchical libraries best explained individual differences in both recall and out-of-sample continuation choices, outperforming all alternative models. These findings cast structured learning as bounded program induction in which the order of experience shapes future abstractions a learner builds.

</details>


### [285] [In LLM Reasoning, there is Irrationality on top of Value Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20624)
*Kejiang Qian,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“理性价值风险”概念，形式化了LLM在推理中无法最大化对齐价值的效用差距，并通过大量实验验证了该风险普遍存在、价值对齐无法消除、对推理策略高度敏感，以及长推理带来递减的理性收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在（后）训练中已与目标价值函数良好对齐，但在实际推理时仍可能无法最大化对齐价值，存在一个未被充分研究的效用差距。

Method: 将理性价值风险形式化为部署的推理策略与理性策略（在最陡方向上最大化期望效用的响应）之间的效用差距，并将估计误差分解为有限候选、有限提示和不完美验证器三个成分。

Result: 覆盖Llama-3.1、Qwen-2.5、Tulu-3（7B-72B）、GPT-5.2、GPT-5.5、DeepSeek-V4及多个基准的广泛实验验证了：(1)理性价值风险普遍存在；(2)价值对齐可以降低但不能消除该风险；(3)该风险对推理时策略高度敏感；(4)更长推理改善理性但收益递减。

Conclusion: 理性价值风险是LLM部署中的一个根本性问题，需要超越训练阶段的对齐，关注推理阶段的理性优化。

Abstract: Significant progress has been made in aligning LLMs with target value functions. We argue that, even when an LLM has been well aligned in (post-)training, it may still fail to maximise the aligned value in reasoning. We mathematically formalise this gap as rational value risk: the utility discrepancy between a model's deployed reasoning strategy and its rational counterpart, which is defined to be the responses that maximise expected utility in the steepest direction. The estimation error of rational value risk is further decomposed into three components from finite candidates, finite prompts, and imperfect verifiers. Extensive experiments are conducted, covering models Llama-3.1, Qwen-2.5, T{\"}ulu-3 families (7B-72B), GPT-5.2, GPT-5.5, and DeepSeek-V4, and benchmarks UltraFeedback, AlpacaEval, GSM8K, MATH, HumanEval, and MathArena. The results validate that (1) rational value risk is widespread; (2) value alignment can reduce, but cannot eliminate, it; (3) the risk is highly sensitive to inference-time reasoning strategy; and (4) longer reasoning improves rationality with diminishing returns. The code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/LLM-Rationality.

</details>


### [286] [AlphaMemo: Structured Search-Process Memory for Self-Evolving Alpha Mining Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20625)
*Hang Yu,Zifan Zheng,Jeff Z. Pan,Tongliang Liu,Zhiyong Wang,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AlphaMemo提出了一种具有结构化搜索过程记忆的自演化Alpha挖掘智能体，通过记录AST差异中的编辑模式、置信门控残差记忆和非对称否决控制，提升了因子发现效率和样本外表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在Alpha挖掘中面临组合搜索空间大、反馈噪声非平稳、发现冗余以及过度使用过去成功经验导致的过拟合风险。

Method: 1) 从抽象语法树(AST)差异中提取编辑模式(motif)；2) 在搜索分类账先验基础上构建置信门控残差记忆；3) 使用非对称否决控制抑制高置信度的失败模式。

Result: 在CSI 500和S&P 500指数上，AlphaMemo在样本外表现和固定预算发现效率上均取得提升；消融实验验证了残差学习、置信门控、AST差异模式和否决记忆各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 结构化搜索过程记忆比单纯记忆最终因子或完整轨迹更有效，能够提升Alpha挖掘智能体的泛化能力和发现效率。

Abstract: LLM agents are promising for alpha mining via combining financial priors, symbolic reasoning, executable factor generation, and feedback-driven refinement. Yet, they face a combinatorial search space, noisy non-stationary feedback, redundant discoveries, and overfitting risks from naively reusing past successes. To address these challenges, we propose AlphaMemo, a self-evolving alpha mining agent with Structured Search-Process Memory. Rather than memorizing only final factors or full trajectories, AlphaMemo records reusable evidence about which edit motifs work or fail under specific parent-factor contexts. It extracts motifs from Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) differences, applies confidence-gated residual memory on top of a search-ledger prior, and uses asymmetric veto control to suppress high-confidence failure patterns. Experiments on CSI 500 and S\&P 500 show improved out-of-sample performance and fixed-budget discovery efficiency, with ablations validating the roles of residual learning, confidence gating, AST-diff motifs, and veto memory. Code is at https://github.com/jarrettyu/AlphaMemo.

</details>


### [287] [Latent Goal Prediction from Language for Model-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20627)
*Samuel Barbeau,Simon Roy,Giovanni Beltrame,Christian Desrosiers,Nicolas Thome*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LAGO通过从语言指令中预测中间潜在子目标序列，并在同一潜在空间中进行动作条件 rollout，结合在线更新和软最小轨迹成本，实现了长程、鲁棒且精确的基于模型的规划，避免了以往方法的性能急剧退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于世界模型的规划面临预测误差累积和优化目标难以定义的双重瓶颈。视觉目标提供精确局部梯度但缺乏远程引导，语言灵活但受限于跨模态对齐噪声或依赖不适合高采样规划的大生成模型。

Method: LAGO框架从语言指令预测一系列中间目标状态（潜在子目标），并在同一潜在空间执行动作条件 rollout。规划时在线更新子目标，并采用软最小轨迹成本进行优化，而非优化单一全局目标。

Result: 在多个环境和规划范围上的评估表明，LAGO避免了先前方法的性能急剧退化，实现了鲁棒且精确的长程规划。

Conclusion: LAGO弥合了视觉目标的精确性与文本引导控制的灵活性之间的鸿沟，实现了纯语言驱动的鲁棒长程规划。

Abstract: Planning with world models is bottlenecked by compounding prediction errors and the difficulty of defining optimizable goals. Visual targets provide precise local gradients but poor distant guidance, while language is flexible yet limited by noisy cross-modal alignment or dependence on large generative models unsuited for the high-sampling nature of model-based planning. To address these challenges, we introduce Latent Goal Prediction from Language (LAGO), a framework that predicts both sequences of intermediate goal states from language instructions and action-conditioned rollouts, all within the same latent space. Rather than optimizing toward a single global objective, LAGO dynamically decomposes instructions into explicitly predicted, locally tractable latent subgoals. By updating these subgoals online and using a soft minimum trajectory cost during planning, LAGO enables an agent to follow coherent latent trajectories over long horizons. Evaluation across multiple environments planning horizons shows that LAGO avoids the sharp degradation of prior methods. By achieving robust and precise long-horizon planning purely from language, LAGO bridges the precision of visual goals with the flexibility of text-guided control.

</details>


### [288] [Human Decision-Making with AI Assistance under Correlated Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20628)
*Yanru Guan,Naveen Raman,Fei Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI辅助决策中，当特征相关时，如何通过推荐测试来平衡短期决策质量与长期人类学习，证明最优策略需遵循"先探索后承诺"的结构，并提出了相应的算法与近似方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类越来越多地在AI辅助下做出决策（如医生遵循AI推荐的诊断测试），当不同特征（如测试结果）相关时，需要平衡短期决策质量与长期人类学习，而现有工作在特征独立时才有效。

Method: 通过理论证明特征相关下平稳策略会导致任意差的性能，证明最优策略必须遵循探索-承诺结构；证明求解最优策略是NP-hard，提出基于动态规划的有限时域最优策略算法，以及短时域规划+平稳后缀的近优近似方法。

Result: 特征相关性越强，探索阶段越长；提出的近似算法能达到近优性能。

Conclusion: 在特征相关场景下，AI辅助决策系统应主动提供多样化测试以帮助人类学习准确的特征系数，而非固定推荐同一组测试，且探索长度需根据特征相关程度调整。

Abstract: Humans increasingly make decisions with AI assistance; for example, doctors may follow AI-recommended diagnostic tests and base their diagnoses on the results. A natural question is which tests should AI recommend to balance short-term decision quality and long-term human learning when different features (e.g., test results) are correlated. While prior work establishes that stationary policies that recommend the same tests repeatedly are optimal when features are independent, we prove that feature correlations lead such policies to perform arbitrarily poorly. Instead, we prove that any optimal policy must follow an explore-then-commit structure; initially, the AI should offer diverse tests so humans can learn accurate feature coefficients, then the AI should commit to a single set of tests, with exploration length that depends on the degree of feature correlation. We prove that computing the optimal policy is NP-hard and derive a dynamic programming-based algorithm that finds the optimal policy for finite horizons. We additionally develop an approximation that plans for shorter horizons and appends a stationary suffix, achieving near-optimal performance. Our empirical results complement our theory by showing that stronger feature correlation leads to longer exploration phases.

</details>


### [289] [Harnessing Agent Skills: Architectural Patterns and a Reference Architecture for Skill-Mediated LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20631)
*Boming Xia,Liming Zhu,Zhenchang Xing,Qinghua Lu,Dino Sejdinovic,Xiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“技能利用”概念，定义从静态技能工件到动态“使用中技能”的架构职责，归纳10个经验驱动模式（5核心+5支持），综合为四层参考架构（供应链、中介、执行控制、证据与反馈），并通过8个系统交叉实例化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体系统广泛使用技能工件来外化行为知识与指导，但缺乏对技能从静态工件到运行时实例（skill-in-use）之间架构职责的系统化理解，亟需一个统一的术语和诊断框架来分析和比较不同系统中的技能利用机制。

Method: 采用经验驱动的方法，从已有系统中归纳出10个架构模式（5个核心模式+5个支持模式），将这些模式综合为四层参考架构（供应链层、中介层、执行控制层、证据与反馈层），并通过8个代表性系统进行交叉实例化来评估架构的有效性。

Result: 提炼出10个经验验证的架构模式，构建了包含四个职责层的参考架构，并在8个不同系统中成功实例化，验证了该架构作为分析技能利用职责的诊断框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该模式与参考架构为分析和设计LLM智能体系统中的技能利用职责提供了统一的词汇表和诊断框架，有助于理解技能工件到使用中技能的转换、执行约束管理以及证据捕获等关键架构问题。

Abstract: Agent skills externalise reusable agent-facing behavioural knowledge and guidance as persistent artefacts that can be discovered, activated, and interpreted by LLM agents. Although a skill artefact is static at rest, its architectural responsibilities arise in use, when the artefact is selected for a run, bound to context and authority constraints, interpreted by a stochastic agent, and recorded as run evidence. We call this run-specific relation skill-in-use. This paper studies agent skill harnessing: the architectural responsibilities that govern the transition from skill artefacts to skill-in-use, bound the executable consequences associated with skill-in-use, and capture evidence for attribution, verification, repair, and evolution. This paper provides a catalogue of ten empirically grounded architectural patterns (five core, five supporting) for skill harnessing and synthesises them into a reference architecture with four responsibility layers: Supply Chain, Mediation, Execution Control, and Evidence & Feedback. We evaluate the architecture through cross-instantiation across 8 selected systems. The resulting patterns and reference architecture provide a vocabulary and diagnostic frame for analysing skill-harnessing responsibilities across agent systems.

</details>


### [290] [DEMM-Bench: A Cross-Regime Benchmark for Agent-Runtime Governance-Evidence Sufficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20634)
*Oleg Solozobov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DEMM-Bench是一个跨机制基准测试，用于评估智能体运行时系统中决策证据的充分性。通过8个证据机制和64个手稿案例，该基准发现仅依赖追踪或日志等方案在75%-50%案例中存在过度声明问题，而基于属性级别的红队评分器实现了零过度声明和56.25%的平均属性充分性准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体运行时系统虽然能生成多种记录（追踪、账本、策略日志等），但这些记录并不足以回答关于特定决策的治理问题。缺乏一个统一的基准来评估不同证据机制能否充分重构决策级属性。

Method: 基于决策证据成熟度模型(DEMM)，通过适配器标准化8个证据机制，提出属性级问题（涉及行为者、权限、动作、策略、决策依据等），并应用8种确定性退化条件进行测试。在64个手稿案例上，对比了追踪呈现、模式呈现、账本呈现等基线方法与属性级评分器的表现。

Result: 追踪呈现和模式呈现基线在75%案例中过度声明，账本呈现在50%案例中过度声明，而红队的属性级候选评分器实现了零过度声明和56.25%的平均属性充分性准确率。

Conclusion: 单纯依赖现有运行时记录格式不足以可靠地回答决策治理问题，需要属性级证据充分性评估。DEMM-Bench提供了可复现的基准测试框架，包含64个案例数据集、构造标注、基线和适配器，支持异构智能体运行时证据基质上的决策证据成熟度评估。

Abstract: Agent-runtime systems emit traces, ledgers, provenance graphs, policy logs, delegation tokens, cache events, and tool-firewall records, but those containers do not necessarily answer governance questions about a specific decision. DEMM-Bench is a cross-regime benchmark for agent-runtime governance-evidence sufficiency, grounded in the Decision Evidence Maturity Model (DEMM): it measures whether records across eight evidence regimes are sufficient to reconstruct decision-level properties rather than merely present. The benchmark normalizes the regimes through adapters, asks property questions over actor, authority, action, policy, decision basis, resource touch, lifecycle context, and verification strength, and applies eight deterministic degradation conditions. Across 64 manuscript cases, trace-present and schema-present baselines overclaim on 75% of cases, ledger-present overclaims on 50%, and the redacted property-level candidate scorer has zero overclaim with 56.25% mean Property Sufficiency Accuracy. The deposited package provides the 64-case dataset, construction-oracle labels, baselines, and adapters, supporting reproducible evaluation of decision-evidence maturity across heterogeneous agent-runtime evidence substrates.

</details>


### [291] [SkillHarness: Harnessing Safe Skills for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20636)
*Yurun Chen,Biao Yi,Keting Yin,Shengyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SkillHarness框架，通过技能边界建模和安全约束实现动态环境中计算机使用代理的安全技能学习与复用，将不安全率降低57.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CUA技能学习方法假设静态安全环境，忽略对抗交互（如提示注入）和环境动态（如弹窗）带来的风险，导致技能学习存在安全风险且执行脆弱。

Method: SkillHarness框架：(1) 技能边界——利用多源监督信号从交互轨迹中识别安全技能；(2) 自改进安全约束——在技能全生命周期中构建并持续优化约束；(3) 选择性技能复用——根据上下文引导任务分解并通过激活技能子集完成任务。

Result: 将学习技能的不安全率降低57.1%，在动态环境变化下持续提升执行稳定性，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 将技能学习与利用建模为安全约束的交互过程，在动态环境中有效保障CUA的安全性和执行稳定性。

Abstract: Computer-Use Agents (CUAs) are increasingly deployed in dynamic interactive environments, creating a growing need for continual skill learning during interaction. Recent approaches address this challenge by learning reusable skills from successful trajectories. However, these skill learning methods largely assume static and safe environments, overlooking risks from adversarial interactions (e.g., prompt injections) and environmental dynamics (e.g., pop-ups). In dynamic settings, such assumptions can lead to risky skill learning and brittle execution, undermining the reliability of CUAs. This raises the question: how can CUAs learn and use skills safely in dynamic environments? To address this problem, we propose SkillHarness, a framework for safe skill harnessing in dynamic environments. SkillHarness moves beyond static skill abstractions by modeling skill learning and utilization as a safety-constrained interaction process. Specifically, we introduce the skill boundary that leverages multi-source supervision signals to identify safe skills from interaction trajectories, and construct self-improving safety constraints throughout the skill lifecycle. In addition, SkillHarness introduces selective skill reuse, where tasks are guided to decompose according to context and completed through the selective activation of skill subsets. Our experiments demonstrate that SkillHarness significantly reduces the unsafe rate of learned skills by 57.1% and consistently improves execution stability under dynamic environmental changes, outperforming existing baselines.

</details>


### [292] [Constituency Optimisation Through Hamiltonian Representation Of Mandates (COTHROM): Algorithmic Redistricting of Irish Election Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20637)
*Ruaidhrí Campion,Matthew Fenlon,Joshua Cooney Mercedal,Casey Farren-Colloty,Eliza Somerville,Michael A. J. Mitchell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对爱尔兰PR-STV选举制度的计算化选区重划框架——COTHRON，利用统计物理（Potts哈密顿量）、MCMC模拟退火和多准则决策分析（MCDA/Pareto最优性）优化比例代表性与紧凑性等宪法目标，并在科克郡实验中优于现有合法选区边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱尔兰PR-STV选举制度中的选区重划面临巨大配置空间下平衡多个相互冲突宪法目标的挑战，缺乏系统化的计算优化框架，现有方法难以量化权衡取舍。

Method: 将选区重划问题解析为统计物理模型，宪法目标作为Potts哈密顿量中的项；使用MCMC方法和模拟退火最小化目标函数，耦合常数作为目标权重的代理；利用多准则决策分析（MCDA）和Pareto最优性解决目标权重组合的模糊性。

Result: 在科克郡的实验中，针对比例代表性和紧凑性目标，COTHRON框架在多种目标权重下均一致优于现有的法定选区边界。

Conclusion: COTHRON为爱尔兰选区重划提供了首个系统性计算优化框架，使宪法目标间的权衡变得明确且可量化，实证表明能生成比现行法律边界更具代表性和紧凑性的选区方案。

Abstract: Electoral redistricting in Ireland's Proportional Representation Single Transferable Vote (PR-STV) system faces the challenge of selecting an optimally representative set of electoral boundaries from an enormous set of possible configurations, and where ``representative'' is a delicate balance of constitutional objectives that are often in tension with one another. We present the first computational framework for Irish electoral redistricting that systematically optimises across multiple constitutional requirements while making trade-offs explicit and quantifiable. The electoral redistricting problem is parsed using statistical physics, where constitutional objectives are considered as terms in a Potts Hamiltonian. Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods and simulated annealing are employed to minimise this objective function, systematically exploring this configuration space, with coupling constants as proxies for objective weightings. Multi Criterion Decision Analysis (MCDA) and Pareto Optimality is then utilised to remedy the ambiguity in choosing a certain objective weighting combination over others. With respect to proportional representation and compactness objectives evaluated in County Cork, COTHROM consistently improves on the existing legal constituency boundaries for a range of objective weightings.

</details>


### [293] [RIZZ: Routing Interactions to Near Zero-Interference Zones for Continual Adaptation of Black-Box Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20638)
*Sonali Goel,Pranav Vaidhyanathan,Lucas Schorling,Natalia Ares,Maike Osborne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RIZZ是一种面向复合语言模型系统的持续适应框架，通过验证器门控记忆、路由和提示编译，在完全黑盒条件下实现在线适应，有效控制干扰并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒适应方法（如单提示优化、无区分记忆、依赖大量搜索）在处理非平稳输入流、稀疏反馈以及跨任务家族的行为污染时存在明显不足，亟需一种能够在线适应、显式控制干扰的持续学习框架。

Method: RIZZ将输入流组织为动态生成的内存分支；推理时通过上下文感知的路由器选择或创建分支，检索分支本地、全局、图结构和工作记忆等多种上下文，编译为有界提示；模型输出后由任务验证器评分，仅已验证的交互可更新记忆、推广/降级规则或创建反模式。

Result: 在竞争性基准测试中，RIZZ框架相较于最先进的基线方法表现出更优的性能。

Conclusion: RIZZ通过验证器门控的记忆与路由机制，在黑盒条件下实现了有效的持续适应，能够显式控制跨任务干扰，证明了其在非平稳、稀疏反馈场景下的优越性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as long-lived agents that must adapt across users, tasks, domains, modalities, and feedback regimes without access to model weights. Existing black-box adaptation methods typically optimize a single prompt, maintain an undifferentiated memory, or rely on repeated rollout-heavy search. However, these designs struggle when streams of input are nonstationary, feedback is sparse, and failures from one task family can contaminate behavior on another. We introduce RIZZ (Routing Interactions to Near Zero-interference Zones), a continual adaptation framework for compound language-model systems that learns entirely through verifier-gated memory, routing, and prompt compilation. RIZZ organizes input streams into dynamically spawned memory branches. At inference time, either while online or offline, a context-aware router selects or creates a branch that retrieves branch-local, global, graph-structured, and working-memory context, which is compiled into a bounded prompt together with retrieved task evidence. After the model acts, task verifiers score the output, and only verified interactions can update memory, promote reusable rules, demote harmful rules, or create anti-patterns. This yields a black-box agent that improves through persistent natural-language feedback while explicitly controlling interference. RIZZ targets the regime where adaptation must occur online under context budgets. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework against state-of-the-art baselines on competitive benchmarks.

</details>


### [294] [An LLM-Explainable DRL Framework for Passenger-Directed Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20640)
*Ouided Braoui,Meriem Bouali,Nadir Farhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将深度强化学习（DRL）与大型语言模型（LLM）相结合，实现自动驾驶的适应性控制与可解释性，DRL代理在仿真中通过Dueling Double Deep Q-Network学习三种驾驶模式（快速、舒适、停止），LLM模块负责解析乘客请求、判断何时需要解释并生成安全导向的说明，结果表明该框架在安全性、适应性和可解释性之间取得了平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于自动驾驶决策过程缺乏透明度，公众信任度有限，因此需要一种既能实现自适应驾驶控制又能向乘客解释代理行为的框架。

Method: 使用Dueling Double Deep Q-Network在仿真中训练DRL代理，使其能够遵循快、舒适、停止三种驾驶请求并实现单次行程中的可靠模式切换；同时引入LLM模块解析乘客请求、判断解释时机并生成简洁的安全导向解释。

Result: DRL代理表现出稳定的学习能力、安全遵守交通规则以及可靠的模式切换；LLM模块能有效生成解释；当请求因安全约束被延迟或覆盖时，框架效果最佳。

Conclusion: 该概念验证框架成功整合了RL决策与LLM，在安全性、适应性和可解释性之间取得了平衡，为提升自动驾驶系统透明度提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles offer the potential for safer and more efficient mobility, yet public trust remains limited due to the lack of transparency in their decision-making. This work addresses this issue by combining deep reinforcement learning (DRL) for adaptive driving control with large language model (LLM)-based explainability modules designed to communicate agent behavior to passengers. DRL agents were trained in simulation using a Dueling Double Deep Q-Network to follow distinct driving requests: \textit{fast}, \textit{comfort}, and \textit{stop}. They demonstrated stable learning, safe compliance with traffic rules, and reliable switching between modes within a single trip. In parallel, LLM modules were introduced to interpret passenger requests, determine when explanations were needed, and generate concise, safety-oriented justifications. Results show that this framework, serving as a proof of concept for integrating RL decision-making and LLMs, balances safety, adaptability, and explainability, and is most effective when requests are delayed or overridden due to safety constraints.

</details>


### [295] [Hypothesis-Disciplined Multi-Agent Automated Formalization of Asymptotic Statistical Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20642)
*Tingzhou Wei,Zeyu Zheng,Ethan X. Fang,Junwei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多智能体协作和假设审计的Lean 4形式化验证流水线，成功完成了渐近统计理论（含参数与半参数模型）的系统性形式化，产物是公理干净、忠实于原文的Lean代码库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近统计理论的形式化极具挑战性，其核心涉及收敛性陈述、渐近展开、泛函分析以及正则性条件，与Lean 4现有(Mathlib)基础设施之间存在巨大鸿沟。现有方法难以系统性处理这类复杂数学领域的形式化。

Method: 提出一种假设约束的Lean 4形式化流水线，由多个智能体协作：一个管理者协调七个专门角色（证明规划、骨架搭建、Mathlib侦察、证明构建、集成、独立审查、审计）。核心方法论是假设约束审计（Auditor智能体），要求每个主要定理的假设和概念层字段都必须锚定于源数学散文、证明为Lean编码适配器、标记为源隐含，或被拒绝为不支持的加强。

Result: 成功构建了渐近统计理论（特别是参数与半参数模型的渐近分布与效率结果）的系统性形式化。Lean开发产物公理干净且忠实于原文，经Lean检查与人工审计，将定理无关的基础设施和统计概念定义与定理特定组装相分离。

Conclusion: 该多智能体假设约束流水线有效弥合了渐近统计理论与Lean形式化基础设施之间的差距，为复杂数学领域的大规模形式化提供了一种可推广的方法论。

Abstract: Asymptotic statistical theory is a challenging domain for AI-assisted formalization: its central results mix convergence statements, asymptotic expansions, functional analysis, and regularity conditions that have a large gap from existing infrastructure in Lean 4 formalization. To address these challenges, we propose a hypothesis-disciplined Lean 4 formalization pipeline built from multiple agents: a manager that coordinates seven specialist roles for proof planning, skeleton scaffolding, Mathlib reconnaissance, proof construction, integration, independent review, and audit. The main methodological discipline is the hypothesis-disciplined audit, implemented by the Auditor agent: every main-theorem hypothesis and concept-layer field must be anchored in the source mathematical prose, justified as a Lean encoding adapter, marked as source-implied, or rejected as an unsupported strengthening. Using this workflow, we build a systematic formalization of asymptotic statistical theory, especially the parametric and semi-parametric models' asymptotic distribution and efficiency results. The resulting Lean development is axiom-clean and source-faithful, with Lean-checked and human-audited proofs of core parametric and semi-parametric theorems organized so that theorem-agnostic infrastructure and statistical concept definitions are separated from theorem-specific assembly. The formalization results are available at https://github.com/junwei-lu/Lean-Asymptotic-Statistical-Theory.

</details>


### [296] [SPARC: A Multi-Agent System for Electrical Circuit Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20643)
*Mushtari Sadia,Zhenning Yang,Umme Habiba Lamia,Nishat Shawrin,Ang Chen,Amrita Roy Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPARC是一个多智能体系统，通过可执行的基于物理的模拟来回答电路图问题，相比基线方法准确率提升高达58%，达到83%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路图问答任务需要复杂的数学推理，这对多模态大语言模型仍然具有挑战性。

Method: SPARC采用多智能体架构，使用LLM智能体来合成、执行和分析模拟程序，将推理过程锚定在可执行的基于物理的模拟中。

Result: SPARC达到83%的准确率，相比基线方法绝对提升高达58%，并支持系统化的错误诊断。

Conclusion: 通过将推理锚定在可模拟的物理仿真中，SPARC显著提升了电路图问答的准确性和可靠性。
